44	// A completed order — delivered / picked up / received, and not declined or

47	export const isCompleted = (o: ConsumerOrder) =>
48	  o.status !== 'declined'
49	  && o.status !== 'cancelled'
50	  && (o.delivery_status === 'delivered' || !!o.collected_at || !!o.received_at || !!o.delivered_at)

52	// An order is "resolved" once it reaches a terminal state: declined, cancelled,

> AGENT

Type-check passes. 

Here's what I changed in `src/components/consumer/OrderCard.tsx`:

The feedback button was gated only on whether the order mapped to a produce listing (`onFeedback && order.produce_listing_id`), so it appeared on **every** order — including pending ones like the two in your screenshot.

I added the completion check:

```ts
const canFeedback = Boolean(onFeedback && order.produce_listing_id && (completed || reviewed))
```

There's already an `isCompleted()` helper that treats an order as completed only when it's delivered / picked up / received and not declined/cancelled. So now:

- **Pending / active orders** → only the 🛟 *Log a Complaint* button shows.
- **Delivered / received orders** → the ⭐ *Rate produce* button appears.
- **Already-reviewed orders** stay clickable (so the buyer can see/edit feedback) even in the history tab.

The two pending orders in your screenshot will no longer show the feedback button — it'll appear once they're marked delivered. Want me to commit this on the current `feat/order-tracking-history` branch?

[Image #1] the client sent me explain one by one in very simple and understandable without technical terms where any one can understand

> AGENT

Here's each card from your client, explained in plain everyday language:

## 1. "Consumer should have the option of saying 'Delivered'. Now we have it in Farmer"
Right now, only the **farmer** can mark an order as "Delivered." The client wants the **buyer (consumer)** to also be able to press a "Delivered" button — so the person who actually receives the produce can confirm they got it, instead of only the farmer saying so.

## 2. "When some action is taken on an order from Today's schedule list or Order list, both lists should refresh, otherwise a stale order is shown in one of them"
There are two lists showing orders — a "Today's schedule" list and a general "Order" list. The same order can appear in both. Right now, if you update an order in one list (say, mark it shipped), the **other list still shows the old status** until you reload. The client wants both lists to update at the same time, so you never see outdated information.

## 3. "Allow the consumer to give a reason for Cancellation"
When a buyer cancels an order, they currently just cancel it with no explanation. The client wants buyers to **type a reason** for why they're cancelling (e.g. "ordered by mistake," "found it cheaper elsewhere"). This helps the farmer understand why.

## 4. "Order details for a Cancelled order do not reflect the correct data"
When an order is cancelled and you open it to view details, the **information shown is wrong or out of date**. This is a bug — the client wants a cancelled order's detail page to show the correct, accurate information.

## 5. "Add a new status 'Acknowledge' for Declined order"
When a farmer **declines** an order, the client wants a new button/status called **"Acknowledge"** — so the buyer can tap it to confirm "Okay, I've seen that this was declined." It's a way for the buyer to close the loop and clear it from their active view.

## 6. "Add a new status 'Acknowledge' for Cancelled Order"
Same idea as #5, but for **cancelled** orders. The buyer gets an **"Acknowledge"** button to confirm they've seen the cancellation, so it can be tidied away.

---

In short, the client is asking for: let buyers confirm delivery (1), keep both order lists in sync (2), ask buyers why they cancel (3), fix wrong info on cancelled orders (4), and add an "Acknowledge" button so buyers can dismiss declined and cancelled orders (5 & 6).

Want me to look into the code for any of these so we can start fixing them?

324	                  <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-900 leading-tight">
325	                    {localizeName(order.produce_name, lang) || '—'}
326	                  </p>
327	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
328	                    {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}
329	                    {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}
330	                  </p>
331	                </div>
332	                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap ${statusColor(order.status)}`}>
333	                  {statusLabel(order.status)}
334	                </span>
335	              </div>
336	
337	              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1 border-t border-gray-100 mt-3">
338	                <div>
339	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Placed</p>
340	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">{orderDate}</p>
341	                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">{orderTime}</p>
342	                </div>
343	                <div>
344	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Payment</p>
345	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">{paymentLabel(order)}</p>
346	                </div>
347	              </div>
348	
349	              {order.pickup_location && (
350	                <div className="pt-1 border-t border-gray-100 mt-1">
351	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Pickup</p>
352	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">📍 {order.pickup_location}</p>
353	                </div>
354	              )}
355	
356	              {(order.payment_status === 'paid' || order.payment_status === 'completed') && (
357	                <button
358	                  onClick={() => setShowReceipt(true)}
359	                  className="mt-3 w-full border border-green-600 text-green-700 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-50"
360	                >
361	                  🧾 {L('View receipt', 'రసీదు చూడండి')}
362	                </button>
363	              )}
364	
365	              {canCancel && (
366	                <button
367	                  onClick={handleCancel}
368	                  disabled={cancelling}
369	                  className="mt-2 w-full border border-red-300 text-red-600 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-50"
370	                >
371	                  {cancelling ? L('Cancelling...', 'రద్దు చేస్తోంది...') : L('✕ Cancel order', 'ఆర్డర్ రద్దు (30 min లోపు)')}
372	                </button>
373	              )}
374	
375	              {canConfirmReceipt && (
376	                <button
377	                  onClick={handleConfirmReceipt}
378	                  disabled={confirmingReceipt}
379	                  className="mt-3 w-full bg-green-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50"
380	                >
381	                  {confirmingReceipt ? '…' : L('✓ Received', 'అందుకున్నాను')}
382	                </button>
383	              )}
384	            </div>
385	
386	            {/* Pickup / Delivery date the farmer scheduled for this order. */}
387	            {order.fulfillment_date && order.status !== 'declined' && order.status !== 'cancelled' && (
388	              <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
389	                <span className="text-2xl">📅</span>
390	                <div>
391	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-green-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
392	                    {order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
393	                      ? L('Delivery date', 'డెలివరీ తేదీ')
394	                      : L('Pickup date', 'పికప్ తేదీ')}
395	                  </p>
396	                  <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-green-900 mt-0.5">
397	                    {new Date(`${order.fulfillment_date}T00:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
398	                      weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
399	                    })}
400	                  </p>
401	                </div>
402	              </div>
403	            )}
404	
405	            {/* Rider tracking + handover OTP — only for rider-driven home
406	                deliveries (a rider is assigned). */}
407	            {riderAssigned && order.status !== 'declined' && (
408	              <DeliveryPanel order={order} />
409	            )}
410	
411	            {/* Status timeline for everything else: self-pickup, courier, and
412	                farmer-shipped home deliveries (Shipped → Received). */}
413	            {!riderAssigned && order.status !== 'declined' && (
414	              <OrderStatusPanel order={order} />
415	            )}
416	
417	            {/* Rate the produce — only once the order is completed. */}
418	            {order.produce_listing_id
419	              && order.status !== 'declined'
420	              && order.status !== 'cancelled'
421	              && (!!order.received_at || !!order.collected_at || !!order.delivered_at || order.delivery_status === 'delivered') && (
422	              <ProduceReviewBox
423	                orderId={order.id}
424	                produceName={order.produce_name ? localizeName(order.produce_name, lang) : L('this produce', 'ఈ ఉత్పత్తి')}
425	                existing={order.my_review ?? null}
426	              />
427	            )}
428	
429	            {/* Decline reason */}
430	            {order.status === 'declined' && order.decline_reason && (
431	              <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
432	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 uppercase tracking-wide">{L('Decline reason', 'కారణం')}</p>
433	                <p className="text-sm text-red-800 mt-1 leading-snug">{order.decline_reason}</p>
434	              </div>
435	            )}
436	
437	            {/* Refund status — shown whenever a refund exists for this order */}
438	            {order.refund_status && <RefundPanel order={order} />}
439	
440	            {/* Payment screenshot */}
441	            {order.payment_method === 'upi' && (
442	              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-2">
443	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
444	                  {L('Payment screenshot', 'చెల్లింపు స్క్రీన్‌షాట్')}
445	                </p>
446	                {!order.payment_proof_path ? (
447	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">No screenshot uploaded yet.</p>
448	                ) : proofLoading ? (
449	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Loading...</p>
450	                ) : proofUrl ? (
451	                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
452	                  <img
453	                    src={proofUrl}
454	                    alt="Payment screenshot"
455	                    className="w-full max-h-96 object-contain rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50"
456	                  />
457	                ) : (
458	                  <p className="text-xs text-red-600">Could not load screenshot. Please refresh.</p>
459	                )}
460	              </div>
461	            )}
462	
463	            {/* Farmer details */}
464	            {order.farmer && (
465	              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3">
466	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
467	                  {L('Farmer', 'రైతు')}
468	                </p>
469	                <div>
470	                  <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-900 leading-tight">🧑‍🌾 {order.farmer.name}</p>
471	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">📍 {order.farmer.village}</p>
472	                </div>
473	                <Link
474	                  href={`/farmer/${order.farmer.slug}`}
475	                  className="block text-center text-sm font-bold text-green-700 underline"
476	                >
477	                  {L('View farm profile', 'రైతు ప్రొఫైల్ చూడండి ↗')}
478	                </Link>
479	                {whatsappHref && (
480	                  <a
481	                    href={whatsappHref}
482	                    target="_blank"
483	                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
484	                    className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
485	                  >
486	                    💬 {L('Contact Farmer', 'రైతును సంప్రదించు')}
487	                  </a>
488	                )}
489	              </div>
490	            )}
491	
492	            {/* Report a problem — pins the complaint to this order automatically */}
493	            <button
494	              onClick={() => setShowComplaint(true)}
495	              className="w-full border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-gray-50"
496	            >
497	              🛟 {L('Report a problem', 'సమస్యను నివేదించండి')}
498	            </button>
499	          </>
500	        )}
501	      </div>
502	
503	      {showReceipt && order && (
504	        <ReceiptOverlay order={order} onClose={() => setShowReceipt(false)} />
505	      )}
506	
507	      {showComplaint && order && (
508	        <ComplaintModal
509	          presetOrderCode={order.order_code}
510	          onClose={() => setShowComplaint(false)}
511	          onCreated={() => setShowComplaint(false)}
512	        />
513	      )}
514	    </main>
515	  )
516	}
517	
518	// Printable payment receipt. Shown as an overlay; the print stylesheet hides
519	// everything except .yff-receipt so "Print / Save as PDF" produces a clean
520	// one-page receipt on both phones and laptops.
521	function ReceiptOverlay({ order, onClose }: { order: Order; onClose: () => void }) {
522	  const { lang, L } = useLang()
523	  const placed = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleString('en-IN', {
524	    day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
525	  })
526	  const paymentRef = order.razorpay_payment_id || order.order_code || order.id
527	
528	  return (
529	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
530	      <style>{`@media print {
531	        body * { visibility: hidden !important; }
532	        .yff-receipt, .yff-receipt * { visibility: visible !important; }
533	        .yff-receipt { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; width: 100%; box-shadow: none !important; }
534	        .yff-no-print { display: none !important; }
535	      }`}</style>
536	
537	      <div className="yff-receipt bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 my-auto">
538	        <div className="text-center border-b border-dashed border-gray-300 pb-3">
539	          <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-green-700">YourFamilyFarmer</p>
540	          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">{L('Payment Receipt', 'చెల్లింపు రసీదు')}</p>
541	        </div>
542	
543	        <div className="py-3 space-y-1.5 text-xs">
544	          <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">{L('Order ID', 'ఆర్డర్ ID')}</span><span className="font-mono font-bold text-gray-900">{order.order_code || order.id.slice(0, 8)}</span></div>
545	          <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">{L('Date', 'తేదీ')}</span><span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{placed}</span></div>
546	          {order.farmer && (
547	            <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">{L('Farmer', 'రైతు')}</span><span className="font-semibold text-gray-900 text-right">{order.farmer.name} · {order.farmer.village}</span></div>
548	          )}
549	          <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">{L('Payment', 'చెల్లింపు')}</span><span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{order.payment_method_detail || (order.payment_method === 'cod' ? 'Cash on Delivery' : isOnlinePayment(order.payment_method) ? 'UPI' : order.payment_method || '—')}</span></div>
550	        </div>
551	
552	        <div className="border-t border-dashed border-gray-300 py-3">
553	          <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
554	            <span className="text-gray-700">{localizeName(order.produce_name, lang)} × {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}</span>
555	            <span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">₹{order.total_price ?? 0}</span>
556	          </div>
557	        </div>
558	
559	        <div className="border-t-2 border-gray-900 pt-2 flex justify-between items-center">
560	          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">{L('Total Paid', 'మొత్తం చెల్లించారు')}</span>
561	          <span className="text-lg font-extrabold text-green-700">₹{order.total_price ?? 0}</span>
562	        </div>
563	
564	        <div className="mt-3 text-center">
565	          <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-800">{L('✓ PAID', 'చెల్లించారు')}</span>
566	        </div>
567	
568	        <p className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 text-center mt-3 break-all">Ref: {paymentRef}</p>
569	        <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-green-700 text-center mt-2">{L('Thank you for supporting our farmers — GoGrameen Team 🌱', 'మా రైతులకు మద్దతు ఇచ్చినందుకు ధన్యవాదాలు — గోగ్రామీణ్ టీం')}</p>
570	
571	        <div className="yff-no-print mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
572	          <button onClick={onClose} className="border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-gray-50">{L('Close', 'మూసివేయి')}</button>
573	          <button onClick={() => window.print()} className="bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800">{L('Print', 'ప్రింట్')}</button>
574	        </div>
575	      </div>
576	    </div>
577	  )
578	}
579	
580	function DeliveryPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
581	  const { L } = useLang()
582	  const ds: DeliveryStatus = (order.delivery_status as DeliveryStatus) || 'unassigned'
583	
584	  const steps: Array<{ key: DeliveryStatus; label: string; sub: string; at: string | null | undefined }> = [
585	    { key: 'unassigned', label: 'Order placed', sub: 'Waiting for a delivery boy', at: order.created_at },
586	    { key: 'assigned', label: 'Rider assigned', sub: 'On the way to farmer', at: order.assigned_at },
587	    { key: 'picked_up', label: 'Picked up', sub: 'Collected from farmer', at: order.picked_up_at },
588	    { key: 'out_for_delivery', label: 'Out for delivery', sub: 'On the way to you', at: order.out_for_delivery_at },
589	    { key: 'delivered', label: 'Delivered', sub: 'Order completed', at: order.delivered_at },
590	  ]
591	
592	  const stageIndex = (k: DeliveryStatus) => ['unassigned', 'assigned', 'picked_up', 'out_for_delivery', 'delivered'].indexOf(k)
593	  const current = stageIndex(ds)
594	
595	  const formatAt = (iso: string | null | undefined) => {
596	    if (!iso) return ''
597	    const d = new Date(iso)
598	    return d.toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
599	  }
600	
601	  return (
602	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-4">
603	      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
604	        <span className="text-base">🛵</span>
605	        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
606	          {L('Home delivery', 'ఇంటికి డెలివరీ')}
607	        </p>
608	      </div>
609	
610	      {/* Handover OTP — visible once a rider has been assigned. Customer
611	          reads this 4-digit code to the rider at the door so the system
612	          can confirm the right person received the goods. */}
613	      {ds !== 'unassigned' && ds !== 'delivered' && order.handover_otp && (
614	        <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
615	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 uppercase tracking-wide">
616	            {L('Read this code to the delivery boy at your door', 'డోర్ వద్ద డెలివరీ బాయ్‌కు ఈ కోడ్ చెప్పండి')}
617	          </p>
618	          <p className="text-4xl font-black tracking-widest text-amber-900 mt-2 font-mono">
619	            {order.handover_otp}
620	          </p>
621	        </div>
622	      )}
623	
624	      {ds === 'delivered' && (
625	        <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
626	          <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800">✓ Delivered</p>
627	          <p className="text-xs text-green-700 mt-0.5">{formatAt(order.delivered_at)}</p>
628	        </div>
629	      )}
630	
631	      {/* Status timeline */}
632	      <ol className="space-y-2">
633	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
634	          const reached = idx <= current
635	          const isCurrent = idx === current
636	          return (
637	            <li key={s.key} className="flex items-start gap-3">
638	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
639	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-green-200' : ''}`} />
640	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
641	                  <span className={`w-0.5 flex-1 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
642	                )}
643	              </div>
644	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
645	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{s.label}</p>
646	                <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
647	                {s.at && reached && (
648	                  <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{formatAt(s.at)}</p>
649	                )}
650	              </div>
651	            </li>
652	          )
653	        })}
654	      </ol>
655	
656	      {/* Delivery address (always visible — confirms what consumer entered) */}
657	      {order.delivery_address && (
658	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
659	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-1">Delivering to</p>
660	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-800 leading-snug whitespace-pre-line">{order.delivery_address}</p>
661	          {order.delivery_landmark && (
662	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-0.5">📍 {order.delivery_landmark}</p>
663	          )}
664	          {order.delivery_pincode && (
665	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">PIN: {order.delivery_pincode}</p>
666	          )}
667	        </div>
668	      )}
669	
670	      {/* Rider contact — only when assigned (and not yet delivered) */}
671	      {order.rider && ds !== 'delivered' && (
672	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
673	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide">{L('Delivery boy', 'డెలివరీ బాయ్')}</p>
674	          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mt-1">{order.rider.name || 'Your delivery partner'}</p>
675	          <a
676	            href={`tel:${order.rider.phone}`}
677	            className="mt-2 w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-blue-700"
678	          >
679	            📞 Call · {order.rider.phone}
680	          </a>
681	        </div>
682	      )}
683	    </div>
684	  )
685	}
686	
687	// Order status timeline for self-pickup and courier orders. Home delivery has
688	// its own richer DeliveryPanel; this covers the pickup and courier lifecycles.
689	// Courier diverges after "Confirmed": Shipped → Received instead of Ready →
690	// Picked up, so a courier buyer sees the right milestones (and dates).
691	function OrderStatusPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
692	  const { L } = useLang()
693	  const approved = order.status === 'approved'
694	  // Shipped lifecycle (Shipped → Received) covers courier AND farmer-shipped
695	  // home deliveries. Rider home deliveries never reach this panel. Self-pickup
696	  // keeps the Ready → Picked up lifecycle.
697	  const isShippedFlow = order.delivery_type === 'courier' || order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
698	  const isCourier = isShippedFlow
699	  const collected = !!order.collected_at
700	  const shipped = !!order.shipped_at
701	  const received = !!order.received_at
702	  // Online orders that have been paid show an extra "Payment received" step.
703	  const paidOnline = isOnlinePayment(order.payment_method)
704	    && (order.payment_status === 'paid' || order.payment_status === 'completed')
705	
706	  const fmt = (iso?: string | null) =>
707	    iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : ''
708	
709	  type Step = { label: string; sub: string; at: string; done: boolean }
710	  const steps: Step[] = [
711	    { label: L('Order placed', 'ఆర్డర్ పెట్టారు'), sub: L('We received your order', 'మీ ఆర్డర్ అందింది'), at: fmt(order.created_at), done: true },
712	    ...(paidOnline
713	      ? [{ label: L('Payment received', 'చెల్లింపు అందింది'), sub: `${order.payment_method_detail || 'UPI'} payment confirmed / చెల్లింపు ధృవీకరించబడింది`, at: fmt(order.paid_at), done: true }]
714	      : []),
715	    { label: L('Confirmed by farmer', 'రైతు ధృవీకరించారు'), sub: L('Farmer accepted your order', 'రైతు అంగీకరించారు'), at: fmt(order.confirmed_at), done: approved },
716	    ...(isCourier
717	      ? [
718	          { label: L('Shipped', 'షిప్ చేయబడింది'), sub: L('Farmer shipped your order to your address', 'రైతు మీ చిరునామాకు పంపారు'), at: fmt(order.shipped_at), done: shipped },
719	          { label: L('Received', 'అందుకున్నారు'), sub: L('You confirmed you received it', 'మీరు అందుకున్నట్టు ధృవీకరించారు'), at: fmt(order.received_at), done: received },
720	        ]
721	      : [
722	          { label: L('Ready for pickup', 'తీసుకెళ్లడానికి సిద్ధం'), sub: order.pickup_location ? `Collect at ${order.pickup_location}` : L('Collect from the farmer', 'రైతు నుండి తీసుకోండి'), at: '', done: approved },
723	          { label: L('Picked up', 'తీసుకున్నారు'), sub: L('Collection confirmed', 'తీసుకున్నట్టు ధృవీకరించారు'), at: fmt(order.collected_at), done: collected },
724	        ]),
725	  ]
726	
727	  // Current = the furthest milestone reached (last step marked done).
728	  let current = 0
729	  steps.forEach((s, i) => { if (s.done) current = i })
730	
731	  return (
732	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-4">
733	      {/* Header — mirrors the home-delivery panel so every order type's tracker
734	          looks the same, just with the icon/label for this fulfilment type. */}
735	      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
736	        <span className="text-base">{order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery' ? '🛵' : isShippedFlow ? '📦' : '🧺'}</span>
737	        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
738	          {order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
739	            ? L('Home delivery', 'ఇంటి డెలివరీ')
740	            : isShippedFlow ? L('Courier delivery', 'కొరియర్ డెలివరీ') : L('Farm pickup', 'ఫామ్ పికప్')}
741	        </p>
742	      </div>
743	
744	      {/* Prominent completion banner once the order is resolved — same style as
745	          the "Delivered" banner on the home-delivery tracker. */}
746	      {(received || collected) && (
747	        <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
748	          <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800">
749	            {isCourier ? `✓ ${L('Received', 'అందుకున్నారు')}` : `✓ ${L('Picked up', 'తీసుకున్నారు')}`}
750	          </p>
751	          <p className="text-xs text-green-700 mt-0.5">{fmt(isCourier ? order.received_at : order.collected_at)}</p>
752	        </div>
753	      )}
754	
755	      {/* Handover code — shown once the order is ready and before it's
756	          collected. The customer reads it to the farmer at pickup. */}
757	      {approved && !collected && order.handover_otp && (
758	        <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
759	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 uppercase tracking-wide">
760	            {L('Show this code to the farmer when you collect', 'తీసుకునేటప్పుడు ఈ కోడ్ రైతుకు చెప్పండి')}
761	          </p>
762	          <p className="text-4xl font-black tracking-widest text-amber-900 mt-2 font-mono">
763	            {order.handover_otp}
764	          </p>
765	        </div>
766	      )}
767	
768	      <ol className="space-y-2">
769	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
770	          const reached = idx <= current
771	          const isCurrent = idx === current
772	          return (
773	            <li key={s.label} className="flex items-start gap-3">
774	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
775	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-green-200' : ''}`} />
776	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
777	                  <span className={`w-0.5 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
778	                )}
779	              </div>
780	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
781	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{s.label}</p>
782	                <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
783	                {s.at && reached && <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{s.at}</p>}
784	              </div>
785	            </li>
786	          )
787	        })}
788	      </ol>
789	    </div>
790	  )
791	}
792	
793	// Refund timeline. Maps the stored refund_status (our manual 'initiated', or
794	// a Razorpay status: 'pending' / 'processed' / 'failed') onto a simple
795	// buyer-facing progression: Initiated → Processing → Credited.
796	function RefundPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
797	  const { L } = useLang()
798	  const rs = (order.refund_status || '').toLowerCase()
799	  const failed = rs === 'failed'
800	  const amount = order.refund_amount ?? order.total_price ?? 0
801	
802	  // current = how far along the 3-step bar we are.
803	  // initiated → 0, pending → 1, processed → 2 (credited).
804	  const current = rs === 'processed' ? 2 : rs === 'pending' ? 1 : 0
805	
806	  const steps = [
807	    { label: L('Refund initiated', 'రీఫండ్ మొదలైంది'), sub: L('We started your refund', 'మీ రీఫండ్ ప్రారంభించాం') },
808	    { label: L('Processing', 'ప్రాసెస్ అవుతోంది'), sub: L('Sent to your bank/UPI', 'మీ బ్యాంక్/UPIకి పంపాం') },
809	    { label: L('Credited', 'జమ అయింది'), sub: L('Reflects in 3–5 business days', '3–5 పని రోజుల్లో కనిపిస్తుంది') },
810	  ]
811	
812	  const refundedAt = order.refunded_at
813	    ? new Date(order.refunded_at).toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
814	    : null
815	
816	  if (failed) {
817	    return (
818	      <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-1.5">
819	        <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-800">
820	          {L('⚠️ Refund failed', 'రీఫండ్ విఫలమైంది')}
821	        </span>
822	        <p className="text-sm text-red-800 leading-snug">
823	          {L(`Your refund of ₹${amount} could not be processed. Please contact support and we'll fix it.`, `మీ ₹${amount} రీఫండ్ ప్రాసెస్ కాలేదు. దయచేసి సపోర్ట్‌ను సంప్రదించండి, మేము సరి చేస్తాం.`)}
824	        </p>
825	      </div>
826	    )
827	  }
828	
829	  return (
830	    <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3">
831	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
832	        <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-purple-100 text-purple-800">
833	          💸 Refund of ₹{amount}
834	        </span>
835	        {refundedAt && <span className="text-[10px] text-purple-500">{refundedAt}</span>}
836	      </div>
837	
838	      <ol className="space-y-2">
839	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
840	          const reached = idx <= current
841	          const isCurrent = idx === current
842	          return (
843	            <li key={s.label} className="flex items-start gap-3">
844	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
845	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-purple-600' : 'bg-purple-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-purple-200' : ''}`} />
846	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
847	                  <span className={`w-0.5 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-purple-600' : 'bg-purple-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
848	                )}
849	              </div>
850	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
851	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-purple-900' : 'text-purple-300'}`}>{s.label}</p>
852	                <p className="text-[10px] text-purple-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
853	              </div>
854	            </li>
855	          )
856	        })}
857	      </ol>
858	
859	      {order.refund_id && (
860	        <p className="text-[10px] text-purple-400 font-mono border-t border-purple-100 pt-2">Ref: {order.refund_id}</p>
861	      )}
862	    </div>
863	  )
864	}
865

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'
4	import { refundPayment } from '@/lib/razorpay'
5	
6	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
7	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
8	
9	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
10	const CANCEL_WINDOW_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000 // 30 minutes
11	
12	// Buyer cancels their own order. Allowed only while it is still pending (the
13	// farmer hasn't confirmed) AND within 30 minutes of placing it. Returns the
14	// stock and, for a paid order, issues a real Razorpay refund — same machinery
15	// as a farmer decline, but initiated by the buyer.
16	export async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
17	  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
18	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in.' }, { status: 401 })
19	
20	  const { id } = await ctx.params
21	  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })
22	
23	  const supabase = createClient(
24	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
25	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
26	  )
27	
28	  const { data: order, error: loadErr } = await supabase
29	    .from('orders')
30	    .select('id, consumer_id, status, quantity, total_price, produce_listing_id, payment_status, razorpay_payment_id, created_at, order_code')
31	    .eq('id', id)
32	    .maybeSingle()
33	
34	  if (loadErr) return NextResponse.json({ error: loadErr.message }, { status: 500 })
35	  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })
36	  if (order.consumer_id !== session.consumerId) {
37	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })
38	  }
39	  if (order.status !== 'pending') {
40	    return NextResponse.json(
41	      { error: 'This order can no longer be cancelled — the farmer has already responded.' },
42	      { status: 409 },
43	    )
44	  }
45	  const placedMs = order.created_at ? new Date(order.created_at).getTime() : 0
46	  if (!placedMs || Date.now() - placedMs > CANCEL_WINDOW_MS) {
47	    return NextResponse.json(
48	      { error: 'The 30-minute cancellation window has passed. Please contact the farmer.' },
49	      { status: 409 },
50	    )
51	  }
52	
53	  // Return the reserved stock.
54	  if (order.produce_listing_id && order.quantity != null && order.quantity > 0) {
55	    try {
56	      await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', {
57	        p_listing_id: order.produce_listing_id,
58	        p_qty: order.quantity,
59	      })
60	    } catch (e) {
61	      console.error('[YFF] restock on cancel failed:', e)
62	    }
63	  }
64	
65	  const update: Record<string, unknown> = { status: 'cancelled', decline_reason: 'Cancelled by buyer' }
66	
67	  const paidByRazorpay = order.payment_status === 'paid' && !!order.razorpay_payment_id
68	  const paidByOther =
69	    order.payment_status === 'completed' ||
70	    order.payment_status === 'payment_claimed' ||
71	    order.payment_status === 'pending_confirmation'
72	
73	  if (paidByRazorpay) {
74	    const amountPaise = Math.round((Number(order.total_price) || 0) * 100)
75	    if (amountPaise > 0) {
76	      try {
77	        const refund = await refundPayment({
78	          paymentId: order.razorpay_payment_id as string,
79	          amountPaise,
80	          notes: { order_code: order.order_code ?? '', order_id: order.id, reason: 'buyer_cancel' },
81	        })
82	        update.refund_id = refund.id
83	        update.refund_status = refund.status ?? 'processed'
84	        update.refund_amount = Math.round(refund.amountPaise / 100)
85	        update.refunded_at = new Date().toISOString()
86	      } catch (e) {
87	        console.error('[YFF] razorpay refund on cancel failed:', e)
88	        return NextResponse.json(
89	          { error: 'Could not issue the refund. The order was not cancelled — please try again.' },
90	          { status: 502 },
91	        )
92	      }
93	    }
94	  } else if (paidByOther) {
95	    update.refund_status = 'initiated'
96	  }
97	
98	  const { error: updErr } = await supabase.from('orders').update(update).eq('id', id)
99	  if (updErr) {
100	    console.error('[YFF] cancel update failed:', updErr.message)
101	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not cancel the order. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })
102	  }
103	
104	  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, refunded: !!update.refund_id })
105	}
106

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
9	  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
10	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ orders: [] }, { status: 200 })
11	
12	  const supabase = createClient(
13	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
14	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
15	  )
16	
17	  const { data: orders, error } = await supabase
18	    .from('orders')
19	    .select(
20	      'id, order_code, produce_name, produce_listing_id, quantity, unit, total_price, pickup_location, status, payment_method, payment_status, refund_status, refund_id, refund_amount, refunded_at, decline_reason, payment_proof_path, created_at, farmer_id, delivery_type, delivery_status, delivery_address, delivery_landmark, delivery_pincode, delivery_alt_phone, delivery_boy_id, handover_otp, assigned_at, picked_up_at, out_for_delivery_at, delivered_at, shipped_at, collected_at, received_at',
21	    )
22	    .eq('consumer_id', session.consumerId)
23	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
24	    .limit(100)
25	
26	  if (error) {
27	    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
28	  }
29	
30	  // Manual join — orders.farmer_id has no FK constraint, so PostgREST can't
31	  // embed the farmer resource for us. One extra query keeps it simple.
32	  const farmerIds = [...new Set((orders ?? []).map((o) => o.farmer_id).filter(Boolean))]
33	  let farmerMap: Record<string, { name: string; slug: string; village: string; phone: string | null; upi_id: string | null }> = {}
34	  if (farmerIds.length > 0) {
35	    const { data: farmers } = await supabase
36	      .from('farmers')
37	      .select('id, name, slug, village, phone, upi_id')
38	      .in('id', farmerIds)
39	    farmerMap = Object.fromEntries(
40	      (farmers ?? []).map((f) => [
41	        f.id,
42	        { name: f.name, slug: f.slug, village: f.village, phone: f.phone ?? null, upi_id: (f.upi_id as string | null) ?? null },
43	      ]),
44	    )
45	  }
46	
47	  // Pull rider contact for any order that's been assigned. Only ever expose
48	  // the rider's name + phone — never id-proof or password fields.
49	  const riderIds = [...new Set(
50	    (orders ?? [])
51	      .map((o) => (o as { delivery_boy_id?: string | null }).delivery_boy_id)
52	      .filter((v): v is string => !!v),
53	  )]
54	  let riderMap: Record<string, { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string }> = {}
55	  if (riderIds.length > 0) {
56	    const { data: riders } = await supabase
57	      .from('delivery_boys')
58	      .select('id, name, phone')
59	      .in('id', riderIds)
60	    riderMap = Object.fromEntries(
61	      (riders ?? []).map((r) => [r.id, { id: r.id, name: r.name ?? null, phone: r.phone }]),
62	    )
63	  }
64	
65	  // The buyer's own produce feedback per order, so each card can show "Give
66	  // feedback" vs the rating they already left. Guarded: if the produce_reviews
67	  // table hasn't been created yet (migration not run), we just skip it rather
68	  // than fail the whole orders list.
69	  const orderIds = (orders ?? []).map((o) => o.id)
70	  let reviewMap: Record<string, { id: string; star_rating: number; review_text: string | null; created_at: string }> = {}
71	  if (orderIds.length > 0) {
72	    const { data: reviews } = await supabase
73	      .from('produce_reviews')
74	      .select('id, order_id, star_rating, review_text, created_at')
75	      .in('order_id', orderIds)
76	    if (reviews) {
77	      reviewMap = Object.fromEntries(
78	        reviews.map((r) => [
79	          r.order_id,
80	          { id: r.id, star_rating: r.star_rating, review_text: r.review_text ?? null, created_at: r.created_at },
81	        ]),
82	      )
83	    }
84	  }
85	
86	  const enriched = (orders ?? []).map((o) => {
87	    const riderId = (o as { delivery_boy_id?: string | null }).delivery_boy_id ?? null
88	    return {
89	      ...o,
90	      farmer: farmerMap[o.farmer_id] ?? null,
91	      rider: riderId ? riderMap[riderId] ?? null : null,
92	      my_review: reviewMap[o.id] ?? null,
93	    }
94	  })
95	  return NextResponse.json({ orders: enriched })
96	}
97

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getFarmerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/farmer-session'
4	import { refundPayment } from '@/lib/razorpay'
5	
6	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
7	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
8	
9	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
10	
11	// Farmer declines a pending order. This runs server-side (not in the
12	// dashboard) because issuing a real refund needs the Razorpay secret. Steps:
13	//   1. authorise the farmer and confirm the order is theirs and still pending
14	//   2. return the reserved stock
15	//   3. if the buyer paid by Razorpay, issue a real partial refund for this
16	//      line's amount and record it; other paid methods get a manual
17	//      'initiated' marker as before
18	//   4. mark the order declined
19	export async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
20	  const session = getFarmerSessionFromRequest(req)
21	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in.' }, { status: 401 })
22	
23	  const { id } = await ctx.params
24	  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })
25	
26	  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)
27	  const reason = String((body as { reason?: unknown } | null)?.reason ?? '').trim().slice(0, 300)
28	
29	  const supabase = createClient(
30	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
31	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
32	  )
33	
34	  const { data: order, error: loadErr } = await supabase
35	    .from('orders')
36	    .select('id, farmer_id, status, quantity, total_price, produce_listing_id, payment_method, payment_status, razorpay_payment_id, order_code')
37	    .eq('id', id)
38	    .maybeSingle()
39	
40	  if (loadErr) return NextResponse.json({ error: loadErr.message }, { status: 500 })
41	  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })
42	  if (order.farmer_id !== session.farmerId) {
43	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })
44	  }
45	  // Only a pending order can be declined. This also makes the call idempotent:
46	  // a double-tap finds the order already declined and refunds nothing twice.
47	  if (order.status !== 'pending') {
48	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order can no longer be declined.' }, { status: 409 })
49	  }
50	
51	  // 1. Return the reserved stock.
52	  if (order.produce_listing_id && order.quantity != null && order.quantity > 0) {
53	    try {
54	      await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', {
55	        p_listing_id: order.produce_listing_id,
56	        p_qty: order.quantity,
57	      })
58	    } catch (e) {
59	      console.error('[YFF] restock on decline failed:', e)
60	    }
61	  }
62	
63	  // 2. Refund if the buyer actually paid.
64	  const update: Record<string, unknown> = { status: 'declined', decline_reason: reason || null }
65	
66	  const paidByRazorpay = order.payment_status === 'paid' && !!order.razorpay_payment_id
67	  const paidByOther =
68	    order.payment_status === 'completed' ||
69	    order.payment_status === 'payment_claimed' ||
70	    order.payment_status === 'pending_confirmation'
71	
72	  if (paidByRazorpay) {
73	    const amountPaise = Math.round((Number(order.total_price) || 0) * 100)
74	    if (amountPaise > 0) {
75	      try {
76	        const refund = await refundPayment({
77	          paymentId: order.razorpay_payment_id as string,
78	          amountPaise,
79	          notes: { order_code: order.order_code ?? '', order_id: order.id },
80	        })
81	        update.refund_id = refund.id
82	        update.refund_status = refund.status ?? 'processed'
83	        update.refund_amount = Math.round(refund.amountPaise / 100)
84	        update.refunded_at = new Date().toISOString()
85	      } catch (e) {
86	        // Refund failed at Razorpay. Don't silently swallow it — keep the
87	        // order pending so the farmer can retry, and surface the error.
88	        console.error('[YFF] razorpay refund failed:', e)
89	        return NextResponse.json(
90	          { error: 'Could not issue the refund. The order was not declined — please try again.' },
91	          { status: 502 },
92	        )
93	      }
94	    }
95	  } else if (paidByOther) {
96	    // Non-Razorpay paid (UPI/manual): flag for manual refund as before.
97	    update.refund_status = 'initiated'
98	  }
99	
100	  const { error: updErr } = await supabase.from('orders').update(update).eq('id', id)
101	  if (updErr) {
102	    console.error('[YFF] decline update failed:', updErr.message)
103	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not decline the order. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })
104	  }
105	
106	  return NextResponse.json({
107	    ok: true,
108	    refunded: !!update.refund_id,
109	    refundStatus: (update.refund_status as string | undefined) ?? null,
110	  })
111	}
112	
```

```
1	-- Order completion flow: courier self-ship + pickup completion timestamps.
2	--
3	-- Adds the two new milestone columns the completion flow needs:
4	--   shipped_at  — farmer marked a COURIER order shipped (parcel handed over)
5	--   received_at — consumer confirmed they received a shipped courier order
6	--
7	-- Pickup completion deliberately reuses the existing `collected_at` column
8	-- (it already means "buyer collected"), and the rider home-delivery flow keeps
9	-- using `delivered_at` — neither is touched here.
10	--
11	-- The `courier` delivery_type is a new value stored on orders.delivery_type;
12	-- no enum/constraint exists on that column, so no type change is required.
13	
14	alter table public.orders
15	  add column if not exists shipped_at  timestamptz,
16	  add column if not exists received_at timestamptz;
17	
18	comment on column public.orders.shipped_at  is 'When the farmer marked a courier order shipped';
19	comment on column public.orders.received_at is 'When the buyer confirmed receipt of a shipped courier order';
20	
```

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
9	
10	// Buyer confirms they received a shipped courier order. Only allowed once the
11	// farmer has marked it shipped (shipped_at set). Stamps received_at, which
12	// resolves the order and moves it to history on both sides.
13	export async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
14	  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
15	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in.' }, { status: 401 })
16	
17	  const { id } = await ctx.params
18	  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })
19	
20	  const supabase = createClient(
21	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
22	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
23	  )
24	
25	  const { data: order, error: loadErr } = await supabase
26	    .from('orders')
27	    .select('id, consumer_id, status, delivery_type, shipped_at, received_at')
28	    .eq('id', id)
29	    .maybeSingle()
30	
31	  if (loadErr) return NextResponse.json({ error: loadErr.message }, { status: 500 })
32	  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })
33	  if (order.consumer_id !== session.consumerId) {
34	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })
35	  }
36	  if (order.delivery_type !== 'courier') {
37	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Only a courier order is confirmed this way.' }, { status: 409 })
38	  }
39	  if (!order.shipped_at) {
40	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order has not been shipped yet.' }, { status: 409 })
41	  }
42	  if (order.received_at) {
43	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order is already marked received.' }, { status: 409 })
44	  }
45	
46	  const { data: updated, error: updErr } = await supabase
47	    .from('orders')
48	    .update({ received_at: new Date().toISOString() })
49	    .eq('id', id)
50	    .eq('consumer_id', session.consumerId)
51	    .not('shipped_at', 'is', null)
52	    .is('received_at', null)
53	    .select('id, received_at')
54	
55	  if (updErr) {
56	    console.error('[YFF consumer/received] update failed:', updErr.message)
57	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not confirm receipt. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })
58	  }
59	  if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {
60	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not confirm receipt. Refresh and try again.' }, { status: 409 })
61	  }
62	
63	  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, received_at: updated[0].received_at })
64	}
65	
```

1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'
4	import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
5	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
6	import Link from 'next/link'
7	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
8	
9	type Order = {
10	  id: string
11	  farmer_id: string
12	  order_code: string | null
13	  produce_listing_id: string | null
14	  produce_name: string | null
15	  quantity: number | null
16	  unit: string | null
17	  total_price: number | null
18	  buyer_name: string | null
19	  buyer_phone: string | null
20	  pickup_location: string | null
21	  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined'
22	  payment_status: string | null
23	  decline_reason: string | null
24	  refund_status: string | null
25	  refund_amount: number | null
26	  refunded_at: string | null
27	  delivery_type: 'self_pickup' | 'home_delivery' | 'courier' | null
28	  collected_at: string | null
29	  shipped_at: string | null
30	  received_at: string | null
31	  fulfillment_date: string | null
32	  created_at: string
33	}
34	
35	type Filter = 'today' | 'week' | 'month'
36	
37	export default function OrderHistoryPage() {
38	  const router = useRouter()
39	  const { tx, L } = useLang()
40	  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Order[]>([])
41	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
42	  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>('week')
43	
44	  const load = useCallback(async () => {
45	    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
46	    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }
47	
48	    setLoading(true)
49	    // Explicit columns: handover_otp is deliberately NOT fetched — the pickup
50	    // code must come from the customer, so the farmer's browser never sees it.
51	    const { data } = await supabase
52	      .from('orders')
53	      .select('id, farmer_id, order_code, produce_listing_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, buyer_name, buyer_phone, pickup_location, status, payment_status, decline_reason, refund_status, refund_amount, refunded_at, delivery_type, collected_at, shipped_at, received_at, fulfillment_date, created_at')
54	      .eq('farmer_id', farmerId)
55	      .in('status', ['approved', 'declined'])
56	      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
57	
58	    setOrders((data ?? []) as Order[])
59	    setLoading(false)
60	  }, [router])
61	
62	  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
63	
64	  // Farmer sets/updates the pickup-or-delivery date on an approved order.
65	  const setFulfillmentDate = async (orderId: string, date: string) => {
66	    const value = date || null
67	    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, fulfillment_date: value } : o)))
68	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ fulfillment_date: value }).eq('id', orderId)
69	  }
70	
71	  const filterStart = () => {
72	    if (filter === 'today') {
73	      const d = new Date()
74	      d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
75	      return d.getTime()
76	    }
77	    if (filter === 'week') return Date.now() - 7 * 86400000
78	    return Date.now() - 30 * 86400000
79	  }
80	
81	  const filtered = orders.filter((o) => new Date(o.created_at).getTime() >= filterStart())
82	  const revenue = filtered
83	    .filter((o) => o.status === 'approved')
84	    .reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0)
85	
86	  return (
87	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
88	      {/* Header */}
89	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
90	        <Link href="/farmer/dashboard" className="text-green-300 text-sm flex items-center gap-1 mb-4">
91	          ← {tx.back}
92	        </Link>
93	        <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">
94	          {tx.orderHistory}
95	        </h1>
96	        <p className="text-green-400 text-sm mt-1">Approved &amp; declined orders</p>
97	      </div>
98	
99	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4">
100	        {/* Filter pills */}
101	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-3 flex gap-2">
102	          {(['today', 'week', 'month'] as const).map((f) => (
103	            <button
104	              key={f}
105	              onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
106	              className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
107	                filter === f ? 'bg-green-700 text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 active:bg-gray-200'
108	              }`}
109	            >
110	              {f === 'today' ? tx.filterToday : f === 'week' ? tx.filterWeek : tx.filterMonth}
111	            </button>
112	          ))}
113	        </div>
114	
115	        {/* Revenue summary */}
116	        {revenue > 0 && (
117	          <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
118	            <div>
119	              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-green-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
120	                {tx.totalRevenue}
121	              </p>
122	              <p className="text-3xl font-black text-green-800 mt-0.5">₹{revenue}</p>
123	            </div>
124	            <div className="text-4xl">💰</div>
125	          </div>
126	        )}
127	
128	        {/* Order list */}
129	        {loading ? (
130	          <div className="text-center py-16">
131	            <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
132	            <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-3">{tx.loadingLabel}</p>
133	          </div>
134	        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
135	          <div className="text-center py-16">
136	            <div className="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
137	            <p className="font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm">{tx.noPendingOrders}</p>
138	          </div>
139	        ) : (
140	          <div className="space-y-3">
141	            {filtered.map((order) => (
142	              <HistoryCard
143	                key={order.id}
144	                order={order}
145	                onSetDate={(d) => setFulfillmentDate(order.id, d)}
146	              />
147	            ))}
148	          </div>
149	        )}
150	      </div>
151	    </main>
152	  )
153	}
154	
155	function HistoryCard({ order, onSetDate }: { order: Order; onSetDate: (date: string) => void }) {
156	  const { tx, L } = useLang()
157	  const isApproved = order.status === 'approved'
158	  const isDelivery = order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
159	  const isCourier = order.delivery_type === 'courier'
160	
161	  const timeStr = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
162	    day: 'numeric',
163	    month: 'short',
164	    hour: '2-digit',
165	    minute: '2-digit',
166	  })
167	
168	  const stamp = (iso: string | null) =>
169	    iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : ''
170	
171	  // Completion line for an approved order: the final milestone + its date.
172	  // Shipped flow (courier + farmer-shipped home delivery): Received (done) ▸
173	  // Shipped (in transit). Pickup: Picked up.
174	  const isShippedFlow = isCourier || isDelivery
175	  const completion = !isApproved ? null
176	    : isShippedFlow
177	      ? order.received_at
178	        ? { text: `${L('✓ Received', '✓ అందుకున్నారు')} · ${stamp(order.received_at)}`, cls: 'text-green-700' }
179	        : order.shipped_at
180	          ? { text: `${L('🚚 Shipped', '🚚 షిప్ చేయబడింది')} · ${stamp(order.shipped_at)}`, cls: 'text-amber-700' }
181	          : null
182	      : order.collected_at
183	        ? { text: `${L('✓ Picked up', '✓ తీసుకువెళ్ళారు')} · ${stamp(order.collected_at)}`, cls: 'text-green-700' }
184	        : null
185	
186	  return (
187	    <div className={`rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden ${isApproved ? 'border-green-200 bg-white' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50'}`}>
188	      <div className="p-3 space-y-1.5">
189	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
190	          <div className="min-w-0">
191	            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
192	              <p className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight">
193	                {order.buyer_name || '—'}
194	              </p>
195	              <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
196	                isApproved ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
197	              }`}>
198	                {isApproved ? tx.statusApproved : tx.statusDeclined}
199	              </span>
200	            </div>
201	            {order.buyer_phone && (
202	              <a href={`tel:+91${order.buyer_phone}`} className="text-xs font-semibold text-green-700">
203	                📞 +91 {order.buyer_phone}
204	              </a>
205	            )}
206	          </div>
207	          <div className="flex flex-col items-end flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
208	            <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 whitespace-nowrap">
209	              {timeStr}
210	            </span>
211	            {order.order_code && (
212	              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold text-gray-400 whitespace-nowrap">
213	                {order.order_code}
214	              </span>
215	            )}
216	          </div>
217	        </div>
218	
219	        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
220	          <span className="font-semibold text-gray-800">{order.produce_name || '—'}</span>
221	          <span className="text-gray-300">·</span>
222	          <span className="text-gray-600">{order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}</span>
223	          {order.total_price != null && order.total_price > 0 && (
224	            <>
225	              <span className="text-gray-300">·</span>
226	              <span className="font-bold text-green-700">₹{order.total_price}</span>
227	            </>
228	          )}
229	        </div>
230	
231	        {order.pickup_location && (
232	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">📍 {order.pickup_location}</p>
233	        )}
234	
235	        {/* Completion status + date (picked up / shipped / received). */}
236	        {completion && (
237	          <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${completion.cls}`}>{completion.text}</p>
238	        )}
239	
240	        {/* Approved orders: farmer sets/edits the pickup-or-delivery date. */}
241	        {isApproved && (
242	          <div className="pt-1">
243	            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-600 block mb-1">
244	              📅 {isDelivery ? tx.deliveryDateLabel : tx.pickupDateLabel}
245	            </label>
246	            <input
247	              type="date"
248	              value={order.fulfillment_date ?? ''}
249	              onChange={(e) => onSetDate(e.target.value)}
250	              className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
251	            />
252	          </div>
253	        )}
254	
255	        {/* Declined orders: show the reason and the refund status to the farmer */}
256	        {!isApproved && order.decline_reason && (
257	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 leading-snug">
258	            <span className="font-semibold">{L('Reason', 'కారణం:')}</span> {order.decline_reason}
259	          </p>
260	        )}
261	        {!isApproved && (order.refund_status || (order.refund_amount ?? 0) > 0) && (
262	          <div className="mt-1 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5">
263	            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-green-800 flex items-center gap-1">
264	              {L('💸 Refund initiated to customer', 'రీఫండ్ ప్రారంభమైంది')}
265	            </p>
266	            <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 leading-snug mt-0.5">
267	              ₹{order.refund_amount ?? order.total_price ?? 0} · reflects in 3–5 business days
268	              {order.refunded_at && (
269	                <> · {new Date(order.refunded_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}</>
270	              )}
271	            </p>
272	          </div>
273	        )}
274	      </div>
275	    </div>
276	  )
277	}

2854	function TodayScheduleSection({ farmerId }: { farmerId: string }) {
2855	  const { tx, L } = useLang()
2856	  const todayStr = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA') // YYYY-MM-DD, local
2857	  const [date, setDate] = useState(todayStr)
2858	  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<ScheduleOrder[]>([])
2859	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
2860	  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null)
2861	
2862	  useEffect(() => {
2863	    let cancelled = false
2864	    setLoading(true)
2865	    supabase
2866	      .from('orders')
2867	      .select('id, buyer_name, buyer_phone, produce_name, quantity, unit, pickup_location, delivery_type, delivery_status, shipped_at, collected_at, received_at, created_at')
2868	      .eq('farmer_id', farmerId)
2869	      .eq('status', 'approved')
2870	      .eq('fulfillment_date', date)
2871	      .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
2872	      .then(({ data }) => {
2873	        if (cancelled) return
2874	        setOrders((data ?? []) as ScheduleOrder[])
2875	        setLoading(false)
2876	      })
2877	    return () => { cancelled = true }
2878	  }, [farmerId, date])
2879

2883	  const markPickedUp = async (orderId: string) => {
2884	    setBusyId(orderId)

2886	    if (res.ok) {
2887	      setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, collected_at: new Date().toISOString() } : o)))
2888	    }
2889	    setBusyId(null)
2890	  }
2891	
2892	  // Courier / farmer-shipped home delivery: stamp shipped_at (order stays open
2893	  // until it's confirmed received). Same API route as the orders list.
2894	  const markShipped = async (orderId: string) => {

2900	      setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, shipped_at: shippedAt } : o)))

2902	    setBusyId(null)

2904

3015	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{L('No pickups', 'పికప్‌లు లేవు')}</p>
3016	              ) : (
3017	                <div className="space-y-2">{pickups.map((o) => <Row key={o.id} o={o} />)}</div>
3018	              )}
3019	            </div>
3020	            <div>
3021	              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-800 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
3022	                🛵 {L('Deliveries', 'డెలివరీలు')} ({deliveries.length})
3023	              </p>
3024	              {deliveries.length === 0 ? (
3025	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{L('No deliveries', 'డెలివరీలు లేవు')}</p>
3026	              ) : (
3027	                <div className="space-y-2">{deliveries.map((o) => <Row key={o.id} o={o} />)}</div>
3028	              )}
3029	            </div>
3030	          </>
3031	        )}
3032	      </div>
3033	    </div>
3034	  )
3035	}

3037	function ListingRowCard({
3038	  row,
3039	  deleting,
3040	  onDelete,
3041	  onEdit,
3042	  onTogglePause,
3043	  onToggleSuspend,
3044	}: {
3045	  row: ListingRow
3046	  deleting: boolean
3047	  onDelete: () => void
3048	  onEdit: () => void
3049	  onTogglePause: () => void
3050	  onToggleSuspend: () => void
3051	}) {
3052	  const { tx, L } = useLang()
3053	  const emoji = row.emoji ?? '🌿'
3054	  const isPaused = row.status === 'paused'
3055	  const isSuspended = row.status === 'suspended_by_farmer'
3056	  const statusLabel =
3057	    row.status === 'available'
3058	      ? tx.availableLabel
3059	      : row.status === 'coming_soon'
3060	      ? tx.comingSoon
3061	      : row.status === 'paused'
3062	      ? L('Paused by farmer', 'రైతు నిలిపివేశారు')
3063	      : row.status === 'suspended_by_farmer'
3064	      ? L('Suspended by you', 'మీరు నిలిపివేశారు')
3065	      : row.status === 'suspended'
3066	      ? L('Suspended', 'నిలిపివేయబడింది')
3067	      : row.status === 'sold_out'
3068	      ? L('Sold out', 'అయిపోయింది')
3069	      : row.status
3070	  const statusColor =
3071	    row.status === 'available'
3072	      ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
3073	      : row.status === 'coming_soon'
3074	      ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
3075	      : row.status === 'paused'
3076	      ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800'
3077	      : row.status === 'suspended_by_farmer'
3078	      ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800'
3079	      : row.status === 'suspended'
3080	      ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800'
3081	      : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'
3082	  // Farmer can pause/resume their own active or sold-out listings (not
3083	  // moderator-suspended or coming-soon ones). Pause and Suspend are mutually

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
9	
10	export async function GET(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
11	  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
12	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Login required.' }, { status: 401 })
13	
14	  const { id } = await ctx.params
15	  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })
16	
17	  const supabase = createClient(
18	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
19	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
20	  )
21	
22	  const { data: order, error } = await supabase
23	    .from('orders')
24	    .select(
25	      'id, order_code, consumer_id, produce_name, produce_listing_id, quantity, unit, total_price, pickup_location, status, payment_method, payment_method_detail, payment_status, paid_at, confirmed_at, razorpay_payment_id, refund_status, refund_id, refund_amount, refunded_at, decline_reason, payment_proof_path, created_at, farmer_id, delivery_type, delivery_status, delivery_address, delivery_landmark, delivery_pincode, delivery_alt_phone, delivery_boy_id, handover_otp, assigned_at, picked_up_at, out_for_delivery_at, delivered_at, collected_at, shipped_at, received_at, fulfillment_date',
26	    )
27	    .eq('id', id)
28	    .maybeSingle()
29	
30	  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
31	  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })
32	  if (order.consumer_id !== session.consumerId) {
33	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })
34	  }
35	
36	  // Manual farmer join (no FK on orders.farmer_id → farmers.id, so embedding fails)
37	  let farmer: { name: string; slug: string; village: string; phone: string | null; upi_id: string | null } | null = null
38	  if (order.farmer_id) {
39	    const { data: f } = await supabase
40	      .from('farmers')
41	      .select('id, name, slug, village, phone, upi_id')
42	      .eq('id', order.farmer_id)
43	      .maybeSingle()
44	    if (f) {
45	      farmer = {
46	        name: f.name,
47	        slug: f.slug,
48	        village: f.village,
49	        phone: f.phone ?? null,
50	        upi_id: (f.upi_id as string | null) ?? null,
51	      }
52	    }
53	  }
54	
55	  // Rider contact (only after a rider has been assigned). Minimal exposure:
56	  // name + phone, nothing else.
57	  let rider: { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string } | null = null
58	  const dbi = (order as { delivery_boy_id?: string | null }).delivery_boy_id ?? null
59	  if (dbi) {
60	    const { data: r } = await supabase
61	      .from('delivery_boys')
62	      .select('id, name, phone')
63	      .eq('id', dbi)
64	      .maybeSingle()
65	    if (r) rider = { id: r.id, name: r.name ?? null, phone: r.phone }
66	  }
67	
68	  // The buyer's own produce review for this order, if they've left one. Drives
69	  // the "Rate this produce" box on the order detail page.
70	  let myReview: { id: string; star_rating: number; review_text: string | null; created_at: string } | null = null
71	  const { data: rev } = await supabase
72	    .from('produce_reviews')
73	    .select('id, star_rating, review_text, created_at')
74	    .eq('order_id', order.id)
75	    .maybeSingle()
76	  if (rev) myReview = rev
77	
78	  return NextResponse.json({ order: { ...order, farmer, rider, my_review: myReview } })
79	}

{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/api/consumer/orders/[id]/acknowledge/route.ts",
  "content": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\n\n// Buyer acknowledges a declined or cancelled order. This stamps acknowledged_at,\n// which moves the order out of the buyer's Active list and into History. Until\n// they tap Acknowledge the order stays active, so a farmer decline / cancel\n// never silently vanishes into history.\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)\n  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const { id } = await ctx.params\n  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  const { data: order, error: loadErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .select('id, consumer_id, status, acknowledged_at')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (loadErr) return NextResponse.json({ error: loadErr.message }, { status: 500 })\n  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })\n  if (order.consumer_id !== session.consumerId) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })\n  }\n  // Only declined/cancelled orders can be acknowledged — that's the only state\n  // where it's needed.\n  if (order.status !== 'declined' && order.status !== 'cancelled') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order does not need acknowledgement.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n  // Idempotent: already acknowledged is a no-op success.\n  if (order.acknowledged_at) return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, acknowledged_at: order.acknowledged_at })\n\n  const { data: updated, error: updErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .update({ acknowledged_at: new Date().toISOString() })\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .eq('consumer_id', session.consumerId)\n    .in('status', ['declined', 'cancelled'])\n    .select('id, acknowledged_at')\n\n  if (updErr) {\n    console.error('[YFF consumer/acknowledge] update failed:', updErr.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not update. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n  if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not update. Refresh and try again.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, acknowledged_at: updated[0].acknowledged_at })\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "// An order is \"resolved\" once it reaches a terminal state: declined, cancelled,\n// delivered (home delivery), picked up (self-pickup) or received (courier).\n// Active = everything else still in progress. Shared so the active list and the\n// history page agree on where each order belongs.\nexport const isResolved = (o: ConsumerOrder) =>\n  o.status === 'declined'\n  || o.status === 'cancelled'\n  || o.delivery_status === 'delivered'\n  || !!o.collected_at\n  || !!o.received_at",
  "new_string": "// An order is \"resolved\" once it reaches a terminal state: delivered (home\n// delivery), picked up (self-pickup) or received (courier). A declined/cancelled\n// order is only resolved once the buyer has acknowledged it — until then it\n// stays in the Active list with an Acknowledge button so a farmer decline/cancel\n// never silently drops into history. Active = everything else still in progress.\n// Shared so the active list and the history page agree on where each order belongs.\nexport const isResolved = (o: ConsumerOrder) =>\n  ((o.status === 'declined' || o.status === 'cancelled') && !!o.acknowledged_at)\n  || o.delivery_status === 'delivered'\n  || !!o.collected_at\n  || !!o.received_at\n\n// A declined/cancelled order still waiting on the buyer's acknowledgement.\n// Shown in the Active list with an Acknowledge button.\nexport const needsAcknowledge = (o: ConsumerOrder) =>\n  (o.status === 'declined' || o.status === 'cancelled') && !o.acknowledged_at"
}

  "new_string": "export default function OrderCard({\n  order,\n  onComplaint,\n  onFeedback,\n  onAcknowledge,\n  onConfirmReceipt,\n  busy = false,\n}: {\n  order: ConsumerOrder\n  onComplaint: (orderCode: string) => void\n  onFeedback?: (order: ConsumerOrder) => void\n  // Acknowledge a declined/cancelled order (moves it to history).\n  onAcknowledge?: (order: ConsumerOrder) => void\n  // Confirm a shipped order was delivered/received.\n  onConfirmReceipt?: (order: ConsumerOrder) => void\n  // The parent is mid-request for this card (disables the action buttons).\n  busy?: boolean\n}) {\n  const { tx, lang, L } = useLang()\n  const reviewed = Boolean(order.my_review)\n  const completed = isCompleted(order)\n  const awaitingAck = needsAcknowledge(order)\n  // A shipped order (courier or farmer-shipped home delivery) the buyer can\n  // confirm. Rider deliveries never set shipped_at, self-pickup is excluded.\n  const canConfirmReceipt = Boolean(\n    onConfirmReceipt\n      && order.delivery_type !== 'self_pickup'\n      && order.shipped_at\n      && !order.received_at\n      && order.status !== 'cancelled'\n      && order.status !== 'declined',\n  )"
}
